Title: Ausbund, das ist: Etliche schöne christliche Lieder, wie sie in dem Gefängniß zu Passau in dem Schloß von den Schweizer-Brüdern und von andern rechtgläubigen Christen hin und her gedichtet worden. Allen und jeden Christen, welcher Religion sie seyen, unpartheyisch fast nützlich. Nebst einem Anhang von 6 Liedern
Creator: Anabaptist (Swiss Brethren) community hymnal, anonymous and named sixteenth-century hymn-writers; with the appended "Confessio, oder Bekänntniß" of Thomas von Imbroich (1533-1558) and "Ein wahrhaftiger Bericht von den Brüdern im Schweitzerland"
Publisher: Lancaster, Pennsylvania: gedruckt bey Johann Bär und Söhnen, "zum siebentenmal aufgelegt in Pennsylvanien", 1856 (year per the host catalogue; the title-page OCR renders the year as "1956", an OCR misreading of the printed date)
Publication Date: 1856
Rights: Public Domain (printed 1856, hymn texts sixteenth century; public domain by date; host carries the Creative Commons Public Domain Mark 1.0)
Language: deu
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_VKZXSla-jKoC, file bub_gb_VKZXSla-jKoC_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, one-world agent for the-anabaptist-movements), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: licenseurl http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/ (contributor Harvard University, Andover-Harvard Theological Library copy, Google-digitised).
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from long-s to s normalisation (7263 occurrences replaced). Rights basis restated: an 1856 Lancaster printing of a sixteenth-century hymnal is public domain by date, and the host marks the item Public Domain.
Content note: A second, cleaner printing of the hymnal already vendored as ausbund_lancaster1846-deu.txt (Johann Bär, 1846, whose Fraktur OCR is garbled). This scan's Fraktur OCR is clean: sampled at five points the verse reads correctly with occasional character errors (Fraktur "f" for "s", "fie" for "sie"), and the whole-file share of clean word tokens is 0.90 against 0.53 for the 1846 file. This file is therefore the witness to quote from, verified against its page images. Contents: Vorrede; hymns headed "Das 1 Lied." onward (numbered hymns with their tune directions and the printed page numbers); the Register of first lines; the CONFESSIO of Thomas von Imbroich; the Bericht of the Swiss Brethren; and the "Sechs schöne geistliche Lieder" appendix. No public-domain English translation of the Ausbund exists (the English selections of 1998 and 2003 are in copyright).
